 釋義



 “AUD”                          Australian dollar, the lawful currency of Australia
「澳元」                            澳大利亞元，澳大利亞法定貨幣

「審核委員會」                         董事會審核委員會

 “Board”                        the board of Directors of our Company
「董事會」                           本公司董事會

 “China” or “PRC”               the People’s Republic of China, which, for the purpose of this report and for
                                geographic reference only, excludes Hong Kong, the Macao Special
                                Administrative Region of the PRC and Taiwan
「中國」                            中華人民共和國，僅就本報告及地區參考而言，不包括香港、中國澳門特別
                                行政區及台灣

 “Company” or “our Company”     Greentown Service Group Co. Ltd. ( 綠 城 服 務 集 團 有 限 公 司 ), an exempted
                                company incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability on 24
                                November 2014
「公司」或「本公司」                      綠城服務集團有限公司，一家於 2014 年 11 月 24 日於開曼群島註冊成立的獲
                                豁免有限公司

 “Director(s)”                  the director(s) of our Company
「董事」                            本公司董事

 “GFA”                          gross floor area
「建築面積」                          建築面積

 “Group”, “we”, “us” or “our”   our Company and its subsidiary(ies) from time to time or where the context so
                                requires, in respect of the period before our Company became the holding
                                company of our present subsidiaries, the business operated by such
                                subsidiaries or their predecessors (as the case may be)
「集團」、「本集團」或「我們」                 本公司及其不時的附屬公司，而倘文義所需，就本公司成為現有附屬公司的
                                控股公司前的期間，指該等附屬公司或其前身公司（視乎情況而定）經營的業
                                務

Company Overview
公司概覽



The Group is a leading happy living service provider nationwide, and is    本集團是中國具領先地位的幸福生活服務商，
based on people’s full range of life scenes and the entire life cycle of   圍繞人的全生活場景及房地產的全生命週期，
real estate, and provides a wide range of services that are strongly       提 供 多 元 化、與 生 活 呈 現 強 關 聯 的 服 務，包
associated with life, which include three types of services, namely        括物業服務、園區服務及諮詢服務三類業務，
property services, community living services and consulting services,      為住戶提供種類廣泛的產品及服務組合，滿
and provide our residents with access to a broad portfolio of products     足客戶、業主及住戶不斷變化的日常生活需求。
and services that address the evolving daily needs of our customers,
property owners and residents.

     property repair and maintenance services, which we refer to               業管理服務的保安、保潔、園藝、物業

     properties or real estate developers management fees for such             服 務 費。於 2025 年 6 月 30 日，我 們 的 總
     services. As at 30 June 2025, we managed properties located in            在 管 合 同 建 築 面 積 達 536.3 百 萬 平 方 米
     197 cities across 31 provinces, direct-controlled municipalities          的物業，涵蓋居民高端住宅、政府基礎
     and autonomous regions across China with a total contracted               建設、商業寫字樓、城市綜合體等，覆

At the mid-point of the year, we are keenly aware that amidst              站 在 年 中 時 點，我 們 清 醒 認 識 到：行 業 變 革

the service density in core areas, constantly improve urban services       源 頭 活 水，推 動 服 務 產 品 迭 代 升 級，將 半 程

iterative upgrading of services and products, so as to effectively         與 合 規 管 理，既 要 滿 意 度，又 要 回 報 率，確
convert the mid-term lead into a full-year dominant position. At the       保高質量發展全線領命，底線不破，高線必爭。
same time, we will strictly control the quality, make breakthroughs

     Acquisition and Future Prospects                                               收購及未來展望
     We have always adhered to the acquisition and investment strategy of           我們一貫秉承「總體堅守穩健同時注重與現有
     “maintaining overall stability while focusing on synergies with existing       業務協同效應」的收購與投資策略。本公司進
     businesses”. At the time of investment or equity cooperation, the              行投資或股權合作時，會重點考慮標的公司
     Company focuses on factors such as the extent to which the target              與本集團業務的契合度、是否能夠提升本集
     company’s business fits the Group’s business, whether the target               團於重點佈局區域的市場份額、標的公司對
     company can increase the Group’s market shares in key regions, the             本集團規模的貢獻程度，以及合作方後續可
     extent to which the target company contributes to the scale of the             能帶來的資源導入等因素；同時關注標的公
     Group, and resources that the partner may bring in the future and              司對生活服務輸出的需求度、能否提升本集
     other factors. At the same time, the Company pays attention to details         團生活服務的覆蓋密度以及有關收購對價等
     such as the target company’s demand for living service output, whether         內容。
     it can improve the coverage density of living service of the Group, and
     the consideration of the relevant acquisition.

     We will focus on the cultivation of strategic core capabilities, focus         我們將聚焦戰略核心能力的培育，專注主營
     on the main businesses, and at the same time strictly implement the            業務，同時嚴格落實投資原則，包括：(i) 於投
     investment principles, including: (i) in terms of investment strategy, we      資 策 略 上，聚 焦 本 集 團 主 營 業 務，加 強 被 收
     will focus on the Group’s main businesses, strengthen the management           購標的公司的經營效益管理，加快虧損項目
     of the operational efficiency of the target company acquired, and              的 止 損 或 處 置；(ii) 於 投 資 方 向 上，以 本 集 團
     facilitate the stop loss and disposal of loss-making projects; (ii) in terms   發展戰略為導向，同時注重與現有業務的協
     of investment direction, we will be guided by the Group’s development          同效應；(iii) 於投資領域上，以本公司現有業
     strategy, while focusing on synergies with the existing businesses; (iii)      務 為 主，新 業 務 為 輔。注 重 投 資 標 的 管 理 能
     in the field of investment, the Company’s existing business shall be           力、業態與區域上與本公司的互補；及 (iv) 於

     attention to the investment target’s management ability, business type         公司業務有質量的擴張，理性審慎投資。
     and regions that are complementary with the Company; and (iv) in
     terms of the investment model, we will focus on asset-light and steady

Foreign exchange risks                                                     外匯風險
The Group conducts substantially all of its business in China, with most   本集團主要集中於中國經營業務，絕大部分
of the transactions conducted in Renminbi. Therefore, the Group is         業務以人民幣進行，因而承受的外匯風險有
exposed to limited foreign exchange risk. During the Period, the Group     限。期 內，本 集 團 未 使 用 任 何 金 融 工 具 作 對
has not employed any financial instruments for hedging purposes            沖用途，也未訂立任何遠期外匯買賣合同以
or engaged in any forward foreign exchange contracts for foreign           作外匯風險對沖用途。本集團將堅持密切監
exchange risk hedging purposes. The Group will continue to closely         察所涉及的匯率風險及利率風險，積極與各
monitor its exposure to exchange rate and interest rate risks and          大銀行探討外匯對沖方案，於有需要時利用
actively explore foreign exchange hedging options with major banks,        衍生金融工具對沖所涉及的風險。
and may employ derivative financial instruments to hedge against risks
when necessary.

to provide a wide range of training and personal development               員工提供多元化培訓及個人發展計劃。向員
programmes to its employees. The remuneration package offered              工發放之酬金待遇按其職務及當時市場標準
to employees is based on their duties and prevailing market levels.        釐定，根據僱員表現支付予僱員酌情花紅，
Discretionary bonuses based on individual performance will be paid to      根據僱員職務及業績表現授予及釐定其期權，
employees, and options will be granted based on employees’ positions       以表揚及回報其貢獻。本集團亦同時向僱員
and performance, as recognition of and reward for their contributions.     提 供 僱 員 福 利，包 括 養 老 金、醫 療 保 障、公
Staff benefits, including pension, medical coverage, provident funds       積金及根據本公司購股權計劃授予的購股權。
and share options to be granted under the Company’s share option
scheme are also provided to employees of the Group.

     enhance the value of the Shares, thereby improving the return to         其股東的整體利益，且本公司的財務狀況穩
     Shareholders, which is in the interests of the Company and its           健、健康，能夠實施股份購回。
     Shareholders as a whole, and the financial position of the Company is
     solid and healthy, which enables it to implement the share repurchase.

persons responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying    審閱的範圍遠較按照香港審計準則進行審核
analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less    的範圍為小，所以不能保證我們會注意到在
in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong             審核中可能會被發現的所有重大事項。因此
Standards on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain       我們不會發表任何審核意見。
assurance that we would become aware of all significant matters that
might be identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
opinion.
